- **gendoc-align-check**: 掃描專案所有對齊層，列出文件↔文件、文件↔程式碼、程式碼↔測試、文件↔測試 之間的所有對齊問題，輸出完整分層報告（只列問題、不修復）。 每個問題標記 CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW，並標示上下游關係來源。 可獨立呼叫或由 gendoc-align-fix 前置執行。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-align-check))
- **gendoc-align-fix**: 讀取 docs/ALIGN_REPORT.md（或先執行 align-check），依指定 layer 自動修復對齊問題。 layer 選項：docs / doc-code / code-test / doc-test / all（預設 all）。 每個 fix 都 verify 確認後才 commit，修復完輸出 Fix Summary。 呼叫時機：user 說「修復對齊問題」「fix alignment」或 align-check 執行後。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-align-fix))
- **gendoc-auto**: 任意輸入→IDEA.md+BRD→移交 gendoc-flow — 支援 idea/圖片/文件/URL/codebase/git 輸入，素材保存至 docs/req/（唯讀原則），生成 IDEA.md + BRD，再由 gendoc-flow 接手純文件生成流水線 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-auto))
- **gendoc-config**: 互動式設定專案的審查強度，並選擇從哪個 STEP 重新開始。 無需手動編輯任何文件，全程對話引導。 呼叫時機：user 說「重跑」「重設」「換嚴格模式」「我想重新開始」 「設定 review 強度」「從第幾步重跑」「不滿意想重來」「清除設定」。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-config))
- **gendoc-flow**: Template-driven document pipeline orchestrator（BRD 備妥後接手）。 支援兩種模式：(1) 常規流程 - 全流水線執行，(2) --only 單步模式 - 執行單一步驟（驗證 input 和 condition）。 從 templates/pipeline.yml 動態讀取步驟定義，對每份文件執行： 專家 Gen subagent → commit(gen) → loop: 專家 Review subagent → 專家 Fix subagent → round summary → commit(review-rN) → check terminate → step summary → state update → total summary（全流水線完成後） Commit 時機：每輪 Review-Fix 結束必有一次 commit（含 finding=0 的 PASS 輪）。 不含硬編碼文件類型；所有文件類型、順序、條件均由 templates/pipeline.yml 定義。 可由 gendoc-auto handoff 觸發，或在 BRD 已備妥時獨立呼叫。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-flow))
- **gendoc-gen-client-bdd**: Client 端 BDD Feature Files 生成 — 智能路由： Web/HTML5 → 讀取 {_TEMPLATE_DIR}/BDD-client.gen.md + BDD-client.review.md 執行 Gen → Review → Fix 三專家迴圈（與 gendoc-flow BDD-client 行為完全一致）。 Unity → Unity Test Framework BDD scenarios（C# NUnit）。 Cocos → Jest TypeScript BDD（features/client/ + tests/cocos/）。 可由 gendoc-auto / gendoc-flow 自動呼叫，或獨立手動呼叫。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-client-bdd))
- **gendoc-gen-contracts**: 從 gendoc 生成的文件中提取機器可讀規格，轉換為可執行合約： 1. OpenAPI 3.1 YAML（docs/blueprint/contracts/openapi.yaml） 2. JSON Schema（docs/blueprint/contracts/schemas/*.json） 3. Pact Consumer Contract（docs/blueprint/contracts/pact/*.json） 4. IaC 模板（docs/blueprint/infra/helm/values.yaml 或 docker-compose.yml + network-policy） 5. Seed Code Skeleton（docs/blueprint/scaffold/src/） 呼叫時機：gendoc-flow CONTRACTS 步驟（所有文件完成後）；或用戶手動執行 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-contracts))
- **gendoc-gen-diagrams**: 從 EDD/ARCH/API/PRD 提取全部 9 種 Server UML 圖，並（條件執行） 從 FRONTEND.md/PDD.md/VDD.md 生成 16 種 Frontend UML 圖， 輸出至 docs/diagrams/（server-: use-case/class×3/object/sequence×N/comm/state×N/activity×N/component/deployment/er-diagram； frontend-: use-case/class×3/object/sequence×3/state×2/activity×3/component/deployment/communication）。 另輸出 class-inventory.md（class→test file 對應表）。 conditions: docs/CICD.md 存在時額外生成 Step 2D — 5 張 CI/CD UML 圖（cicd-pipeline-sequence / cicd-pr-gate-activity / infra-local-topology / developer-workflow-activity / cicd-secret-flow）。永遠生成 Step 2E-MODULITH — 2 張 Spring Modulith 圖（moduli… ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-diagrams))
- **gendoc-gen-html**: 將 docs/*.md 和 features/*.feature 動態掃描並一對一轉換為專業 HTML 文件網站（docs/pages/）。 Mermaid 圖表直接渲染，深色頂導覽列 + 淺色內容區 + 中文字型，Prism.js 語法高亮，RWD。 Mode 1（預設）：全產生 — README.md + HTML；Mode 2：僅 HTML。 可獨立呼叫或由 gendoc-auto 自動呼叫。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-html))
- **gendoc-gen-mock**: 根據 docs/API.md 與 docs/SCHEMA.md 生成 FastAPI Mock Server。 輸出至 docs/blueprint/mock/（可整個目錄帶走）。 包含：main.py（所有 endpoint 1:1 對應 API.md）、requirements.txt、 data/*.json（擬真假資料）、MOCK_SERVER_GUIDE.md（前端工程師使用手冊）。 呼叫時機：gendoc-flow MOCK 步驟；或用戶手動執行。 condition: client_type != api-only ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-mock))
- **gendoc-gen-prototype**: 從工程文件自動生成互動式 HTML Prototype，輸出至 docs/pages/prototype/。支援兩種模式： UI Prototype（PRD/PDD/VDD/FRONTEND/AUDIO/ANIM → 可點擊畫面 + 動畫音效） API Explorer（API.md/SCHEMA.md → Postman 式試打介面，JavaScript 模擬回應，含 deep-link 分享） 兩種文件同時存在時並行生成（full 模式）。含 gen→review→fix loop，commit，並自動更新 README.md。 可獨立呼叫（/gendoc-gen-prototype）或由 gendoc-gen-html 自動呼叫。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-prototype))
- **gendoc-guard**: 監控任意 skill 執行，session 中斷時自動排隊重新喚起，並透過 SECS 白名單攔截未授權工具呼叫。用法：/gendoc-guard &lt;skill-name&gt; ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-guard))
- **gendoc-rebuild-templates**: 全量重建 templates/ 目錄下所有 *.review.md（審查標準）、驗證 *.gen.md（生成規則） 與審查 *.md（結構模板）。依文件管線順序、平行派送 Agent 執行，確保每份 review.md ≥ 20 項、≥ 4 層、有 Check/Risk/Fix 格式。 呼叫時機： - 新增文件類型後需同步建立 review.md - 審查標準老化需全量刷新 - 重大 pipeline 架構調整後重新驗證累積上游 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-rebuild-templates))
- **gendoc-refresh-docs**: 掃描目前目錄實況 + 讀取 Claude memory（project/reference 類型）+ git log， 與現有 README.md / docs/PRD.md 比對，找出過時或遺漏的內容並修正， 再將使用者 args（若有）優化後整合進去，最後選擇性重建 HTML。 適用任意目錄；不假設固定結構；根據 README/PRD 是否存在自動路由。 支援 args：/gendoc-refresh-docs &lt;文字&gt; 提供本次想補記的內容。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-refresh-docs))
- **gendoc-repair**: 把不完整的目標專案補到與 gendoc-auto + gendoc-flow 從頭執行完全一致的狀態。三部分內容驅動 Gate：① pre-DRYRUN 文件品質（存在 + 章節數 ≥ template）② .gendoc-rules/*.json 存在 ③ post-DRYRUN 輸出存在性 — 三者全 ✅ 才進 Branch B，否則 Branch A 修補後重跑 DRYRUN。State 只作參考，不作 Gate 判斷依據。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-repair))
- **gendoc-shared**: gendoc 共用邏輯執行入口。被 Skill tool 呼叫時，直接執行 §-1 R-01（state file 守衛）： 檢查 state file 是否存在，不存在則呼叫 gendoc-config 完整執行所有步驟。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-shared))
- **gendoc-upgrade**: 手動升級 gendoc skills（git pull + re-deploy）。 呼叫時機：user 說「升級 gendoc」「gendoc 更新」「update gendoc」「/gendoc-upgrade」。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-upgrade))
- **gene**: Manage the gene pool — compounding library of proven skill improvement strategies. Use when user asks to view genes, check gene pool, apply a gene, or /gene. ([Jack5316/Skills_Snapshot](https://github.com/Jack5316/Skills_Snapshot/tree/HEAD/gene))
- **gene**: Genetic profile advisor — health, learning, fitness questions from vault. ([Warren-Githut/personal-os-automation](https://github.com/Warren-Githut/personal-os-automation/tree/HEAD/personal_vault/.scripts/skills/gene) · 2 copies)
- **Gene Enrichment Analysis (ORA / GSEA)**: 对基因列表或差异表达结果执行 KEGG 和 GO 富集分析。 自动识别输入格式，根据是否提供排序指标（logFC 等）动态切换 ORA 或 GSEA 模式。 支持多物种、自动 ID 类型探测（Symbol / Ensembl / Entrez）， 并输出散点图、山脊联合图、GSEA 曲线图、Barcode 综合图等高质量可视化结果。 ([Yuanwei-source/Bioskills](https://github.com/Yuanwei-source/Bioskills/tree/HEAD/Enrichment))
- **Gene Panel Selection Workflow**: End-to-end workflow for gene panel design in scRNA-seq and spatial transcriptomics, that should be **STRICTLY** followed: dataset understanding + smart downsampling + train/test splits, algorithmic selection (HVG/DE/RF/scGeneFit/SpaPROS), optimal sub-panel discovery (ARI vs size), biological completion with a stability gate (Completion Rule), consensus scoring and completion (only if there is still room), and benchmarking on test splits (ARI/NMI/Silhouette + UMAP similarity). ([aristoteleo/PantheonOS](https://github.com/aristoteleo/PantheonOS/tree/HEAD/pantheon/factory/templates/skills/omics/gene_panel_selection) · ★497 · 3 copies)
- **Gene Regulatory Network Inference (pySCENIC)**: Infer gene regulatory networks (GRNs) de novo from single-cell RNA-seq data using pySCENIC. This workflow discovers transcription factor (TF) regulons directly from expression patterns and calculates cell-level TF activity scores. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/omicsclaw/grn-pyscenic) · ★224 · 7 copies)
- **Gene Therapy Scientist Expert Agent**: Specialized expert agent operating as a Gene Therapy Scientist with full theoretical and practical domain reasoning. ([Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6](https://github.com/Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-gene-therapy-scientist))
- **gene-absence-confidence-statistics**: Use when a presence/absence analysis calls a gene absent in lineages represented mainly by draft bins — especially when completeness estimates (e.g. ≥75% single-copy genes, ≤1.125 copies) are available and you must decide if the absence call is trustworthy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-absence-confidence-statistics))
- **gene-absence-presence-matrix-modeling**: Use when you have binary gene cluster absence/presence matrices plus literature-based lifestyle labels and need to train lifestyle classifiers or predict lifestyles of genomes from species with no representative in the training set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-absence-presence-matrix-modeling))
- **gene-abundance-and-prevalence-quantification**: Use when a set of clustered gene representatives must be prioritized by how abundant and how widespread they are across a defined collection of metagenomes, e.g. to select representative amoA-AOB clusters from soil metagenomes for primer design. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-abundance-and-prevalence-quantification))
- **gene-accessibility-aggregation-from-atac-peaks**: Use when matched peak-level scATAC-Seq counts must be summarized to per-gene accessibility (3kb-upstream+gene-body aggregation around RNA-defined marker genes) to validate primed states or compare chromatin opening across cell groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-accessibility-aggregation-from-atac-peaks))
- **gene-activity-score-integration**: Use when snRNA-seq and snATAC-seq come from unpaired samples and you need a shared low-dimensional representation before cell pairing or downstream GRN inference; the ATAC assay must be converted to a gene activity matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-activity-score-integration))
- **gene-age-class-count-summarization**: Use when you already have a gene-to-gene-age-class orthomap table for a focal species (e.g. from oggmap of2orthomap on OrthoFinder output) and need per-age-class gene counts for quality checking or downstream plotting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-age-class-count-summarization))
- **gene-age-class-relative-expression-normalization**: Use when a TEI-annotated AnnData (h5ad) with gene age class annotations is available and you need a table of relative expression shares per gene age class per observation group (cell type, timepoint). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-age-class-relative-expression-normalization))
- **gene-age-orthomap-matching**: Use when an AnnData/scRNA object must be annotated with per-gene evolutionary ages (an orthomap) before computing a transcriptome evolutionary index (TEI), e.g. reproducing hourglass or young-gene-peak patterns across embryo stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-age-orthomap-matching))
- **gene-age-weighted-expression-analysis**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq AnnData objects need an evolutionary age metric (TEI or partial TEI profiles) per cell, especially for large datasets (~90,000+ up to 1e6 cells) where per-cell computation must stay tractable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-age-weighted-expression-analysis))
- **gene-alias-resolution-against-hgnc**: Use when RNA-seq data are keyed by Ensembl gene IDs but a downstream biomolecular network model expects gene symbols, and direct symbol matches fail for some genes requiring GeneCards/HGNC alias lookup. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-alias-resolution-against-hgnc))
- **gene-alteration-proportion-zscoring**: Use when building the CRIT feature matrix for candidate genes and you have per-sample TCGA mutation (data_mutations_extended.txt) and CNV (data_CNA.txt) files that must be dichotomized into per-gene alteration proportions and Z-scored. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-alteration-proportion-zscoring))
- **gene-annotation**: Use gget to discover Ensembl reference FTPs, search gene or transcript annotations, inspect Ensembl/WormBase/FlyBase IDs, and retrieve nucleotide or UniProt amino-acid FASTA sequences. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/gget/sub-skills/gene-annotation) · ★357)
- **gene-annotation-column-preservation**: Use when mean-centering or otherwise transforming a COnTORT-style expression matrix where the first five columns are GenBank annotations and must survive transformation unchanged, including 'N/A' blanks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-column-preservation))
- **gene-annotation-expression-matching**: Use when normalizing expression data from multiple GEO series for one organism: expression rows must be filtered to genes present in a GenBank annotation file and joined across experiments by locus tag/gene name. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-expression-matching))
- **gene-annotation-gtf-processing**: Use when preparing a reference for splicing-status prediction of scRNA-seq reads: you have a gene annotation GTF and matching genome FASTA and need an augmented spliced+unspliced transcriptome (spliceu.gtf, spliceu.fa, t2g_3col.tsv). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-gtf-processing))
- **gene-annotation-overlap-classification-of-tars**: Use when you have groHMM-predicted TAR BED files from scRNA-seq alignments and need to partition them into aTARs vs uTARs relative to a refFlat annotation, or quantify the fraction of reads assigned to uTARs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-overlap-classification-of-tars))
- **gene-annotation-skill**: Provide deterministic gene-to-set membership over the PerturbAI closed gene universe. Use for curated expected-similar groups such as paralog families, complexes, pathways, and functional classes; compare those priors with observed findings for divergence, non-interchangeability, specificity, or deterministic attribute lookup. ([jinlabneurogenomics/distributed_agent](https://github.com/jinlabneurogenomics/distributed_agent/tree/HEAD/src/distributed_agents/skills/dataset/gene-annotation-skill) · ★40)
- **gene-annotation-strand-orientation-mapping**: Use when you have strand-annotated BED files of motifs (e.g. TFBSs) and gene regions and need to test whether motif orientation is biased relative to transcriptional direction within transcribed regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-strand-orientation-mapping))
- **gene-annotation-te-repeats**: Use when annotating repeats, transposable elements, and genes on a eukaryotic assembly, or transferring an annotation. Not for building the assembly (genome-assembly-and-curation) or choosing a public annotation (reference-genome-selection). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/gene-annotation-te-repeats))
- **gene-annotation-trainability-scoring**: Use when you have single-cell AnnData with per-cell confidence scores from DNN training-dynamics analysis and want to rank genes by their positive or negative association with annotation trainability (e.g., disease-related genes in mSTZ β cells). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-annotation-trainability-scoring))
- **gene-annotations**: Bidirectional gene ↔ GO term annotation lookups: GO term → genes (child-propagated by default; --direct overrides), gene → GO terms. Sources: GOA via QuickGO at EBI, NCBI gene2go. Use for "GO terms for BRCA1", "genes annotated to GO:0006915", "genes in apoptosis GO term", "GO annotations for TP53". Distinct from ontology-terms (ontology structure) and gene-set-fetch (named catalogs). ([ppavlidis/skillz](https://github.com/ppavlidis/skillz/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-annotations))
- **gene-ase-profile-class-verification**: Use when per-cell allelic state posteriors (P/B/M) from a beta-binomial mixture on F1-hybrid scRNA-Seq cells are available and genes must be assigned to seven ASE profile classes and validated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-ase-profile-class-verification))
- **gene-barcode-matrix-harmonization**: Use when comparing gene-barcode matrices from two scRNA-seq processing pipelines (e.g., UniverSC vs Cell Ranger/dropSeqPipe/CogentAP/zUMIs) before correlation, clustering, or integration analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-barcode-matrix-harmonization))
- **gene-barcode-matrix-reconstruction**: Use when raw scRNA-seq FASTQ/BAM reads (e.g. from SRA SRP073767 or 10x datasets site) must be converted into gene-cell-barcode matrices, or when matrices from different preparations must be regenerated under identical counting settings before comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-barcode-matrix-reconstruction))
- **gene-based-association-result-processing**: Use when Stage II TWAS output from testing.py (per-gene Z-scores from five eQTL-weight training models) must be calibrated via genomic control so median test p values equal 0.5 before ACAT-O aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-based-association-result-processing))
- **gene-based-similarity-screening**: Use when constructing a non-redundant species pan-genome reference from predicted genes, or when assigning human-microbiome metagenomic contigs to reference taxa by -match gene similarity to pick a gene prediction model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-based-similarity-screening))
- **gene-bench-eval**: This benchmark evaluates how well various deep learning models encode biological knowledge by predicting 312 ground-truth gene properties. It probes capabilities across five domains: genomic, regulatory, localization, biological processes, and protein features, using binary, multi-label, and multi-class classification tasks. Use when the user wants to benchmark on GeneBench, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports AUC. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/gene-bench-eval) · ★3)
- **gene-bench-experience-control**: Strategy Gene methodology for experience-driven test-time control in LLM agents. Compact control-oriented experience representation (~230 tokens) outperforms documentation-heavy Skill (~2500 tokens) by +3.0pp. Core principle: encode experience as control signal, not documentation. Includes GEP protocol for gene evolution, AVOID directive patterns, and selective experience accumulation. Trigger: experience reuse, test-time control, skill representation, agent memory, experience evolution, strategy gene, GEP, prompt engineering for agents. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/healthcare-bio/gene-bench-experience-control) · ★4 · 6 copies)
- **gene-biomarker-survival-association-testing**: Use when TCGA-style gene expression matrices are paired with patient survival metadata (vital status, days to death, days to follow-up) and candidate gene sets (e.g., Extracted/IC_&lt;tumor&gt;.csv vs DE genes) must be screened for significant survival association and compared by Concordance statistic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-biomarker-survival-association-testing))
- **gene-biopython**: Computational molecular biology in Python via Biopython. Use when the user needs DNA/RNA/protein sequence manipulation, translation, motifs, parsing FASTA/GenBank/PDB files, programmatic NCBI Entrez/PubMed/BLAST access, phylogenetics, or building custom bioinformatics pipelines. Triggers include 'Biopython', 'SeqIO', 'Entrez', 'translate sequence', 'parse FASTA/GenBank', 'BLAST', 'NCBI', 'phylogenetic tree'. ([oscar-wang-xin/my-skills](https://github.com/oscar-wang-xin/my-skills/tree/HEAD/gene-biopython))
- **gene-body-coverage**: Use when computing gene body coverage curves from BAM files. Optimized Python script with fetch+blocks, numpy binning, max normalization, and multiprocessing. ([ShiganLuo/skill](https://github.com/ShiganLuo/skill/tree/HEAD/data-science/gene-body-coverage))
- **gene-body-coverage-bias-verification**: Use when RNA-seq BAM files from multiple tissue groups (e.g. broiler liver, abdominal fat, breast muscle) must be checked for systematic gene-body coverage bias before variant or expression analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-body-coverage-bias-verification))
- **gene-body-coverage-computation**: Use when you have per-cell BAM files split from a 10x Genomics barcoded BAM and need normalized gene body coverage profiles (100 values per cell) as input to SkewC quality assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-body-coverage-computation))
- **gene-body-coverage-data-loading**: Use when starting the SkewC R Markdown analysis chain on gene body coverage output (e.g. coverage.r) and a compact per-cell percentile-coverage matrix (Coverage_means_DF) is needed for plotting or trim clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-body-coverage-data-loading))
- **gene-body-coverage-skewness-classification**: Use when you have per-cell gene body coverage data (Coverage_means_DF_Clust derived from coverage.r in the SkewC workflow) and need to partition cells into typical vs. skewed groups for quality filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-body-coverage-skewness-classification))
- **gene-bulk-rnaseq**: Bulk RNA-seq differential expression analysis end to end. Use to design and run the workflow FASTQC -&gt; trim -&gt; align (STAR/HISAT2) -&gt; quantify (featureCounts/Salmon) -&gt; differential expression (DESeq2/edgeR/limma) -&gt; enrichment (GO/KEGG/GSEA), including batch-effect correction and expression-matrix normalization. Triggers include 'RNA-seq', 'differential expression', 'DESeq2', 'edgeR', 'limma', 'STAR', 'featureCounts', 'Salmon', 'count matrix', 'bulk transcriptomics', 'batch effect'. ([oscar-wang-xin/my-skills](https://github.com/oscar-wang-xin/my-skills/tree/HEAD/gene-bulk-rnaseq))
- **gene-burden-evidence**: Assess rare-variant gene burden evidence with explicit cohort, ancestry, mask, frequency threshold, model, phenotype, and multiple-testing semantics. Use for gene-level human genetic support and locus-to-gene follow-up. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/gene-burden-evidence) · ★4)
- **gene-by-gene-allele-profile-filtering**: Use when you have a chewBBACA AlleleCall results_alleles.tsv profile matrix over a strain collection and need to define the core-genome (cgMLST) locus set at a presence threshold such as 95%. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-by-gene-allele-profile-filtering))
- **gene-catalogue-expansion-quantification**: Use when comparing a newly built non-redundant gene catalogue against a published one (e.g. VMGC-90 vs VIRGO-90) to report bidirectional coverage fractions and protein-space expansion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-catalogue-expansion-quantification))
- **gene-catalogue-mapping-rate-quantification**: Use when benchmarking the read-capture efficiency of a gene catalogue (e.g., VMGC-90 vs VIRGO) by aligning clean metagenomic reads and computing per-sample mapping rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-catalogue-mapping-rate-quantification))
- **gene-category-expression-profiling**: Use when a per-gene scalar metric (e.g. nucleotide diversity, Tajima's D, F-statistics) is available and you want a cell-level weighted expression index using those bins as weights in place of evolutionary gene age classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-category-expression-profiling))
- **gene-cca-matrix-to-image-conversion**: Use when RNMF has produced gene CCA abundance matrices for genes under fixed mutational signatures (e.g. New, SBS3*, SBS17b*) and the goal is to render them as images for CNN-based classification or prognostic prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-cca-matrix-to-image-conversion))
- **gene-cell-nonzero-fraction-filtering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. h5 expression files for the scGCL pipeline) must be quality-controlled before normalization and graph construction, and a scGNN-style &gt;1% non-zero expression filter is the desired gene/cell exclusion criterion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-cell-nonzero-fraction-filtering))
- **gene-centric-sparse-storage**: Use when a large single-cell expression matrix (AnnData/H5AD) must be persisted in a relational database so per-gene and per-cell-type expression queries run efficiently server-side. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-centric-sparse-storage))
- **gene-cluster-absence-presence-matrix-generation**: Use when starting from quality-filtered bacterial genome FASTAs and needing a gene-cluster-by-genome binary matrix as the foundation for pan-genome analyses or lifestyle-associated gene (LAG/pLAG) prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-cluster-absence-presence-matrix-generation))
- **gene-cluster-abundance-benchmarking**: Use when validating a metagenomic gene-cluster abundance tool against simulated libraries with known abundances, or when choosing a redundancy-filtering cutoff and read-mapping mode for BiG-MAP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-abundance-benchmarking))
- **gene-cluster-adjacency-graph-construction**: Use when you have VAMB-derived gene cluster assignments plus ORF sequences retaining per-contig gene ordering, and you want to group gene clusters into synteny clusters based on genomic adjacency to infer shared pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-cluster-adjacency-graph-construction))
- **gene-cluster-coverage-quantification**: Use when you need to benchmark primer-set sensitivity against a curated reference gene set, e.g., recomputing the 94% (MetaFunPrimer) vs 49% (published) amoA-AOB coverage or per-cluster coverage like cluster 3 at 96% vs 19%. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-cluster-coverage-quantification))
- **gene-cluster-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when you have RPKM-normalized gene cluster abundance or expression tables plus sample grouping metadata and need to identify MGCs/GCFs significantly differentially abundant or expressed between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-differential-abundance-testing))
- **gene-cluster-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when you have antiSMASH or gutSMASH cluster predictions from a reference genome collection and paired shotgun metagenomic/metatranscriptomic reads, and need to test which gene clusters differ in abundance or expression between phenotypes (e.g. Crohn's disease vs healthy). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-differential-expression-analysis))
- **gene-cluster-expression-heatmap-visualization**: Use when BiG-MAP RPKM abundance/expression tables for grouped samples (e.g. healthy vs. caries, CD vs. UC) have been differentially tested and you need a heatmap of significant gene cluster profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-expression-heatmap-visualization))
- **gene-cluster-family-clustering**: Use when antiSMASH v4 GenBank BGC predictions (optionally with MIBiG reference BGCs) need to be grouped into gene cluster families or clans, or when a BGC sequence similarity network must be built and clustered. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-family-clustering))
- **gene-cluster-family-formation-and-similarity-clustering**: Use when you have antiSMASH v5.0.0 BGC predictions from a set of microbial genomes and you need to integrate those predictions with GNPS metabolomic data (MS2 spectra and molecular families). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gene-cluster-family-formation-and-similarity-clustering) · 3 copies)
- **gene-cluster-family-grouping**: Use when SMASH-predicted gene clusters (antiSMASH or gutSMASH GenBank output folders for a genome collection) contain redundant clusters that must be dereplicated and grouped into GCFs before metagenomic/metatranscriptomic read mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-family-grouping))
- **gene-cluster-family-method-benchmarking**: Use when choosing or validating the alignment backend for BiG-SCAPE's DSS index on a growing BGC dataset — e.g. to confirm that Pfam profile-HMM alignment (hmmalign) matches Muscle/MAFFT-based GCF calling while being faster as input BGC count increases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-family-method-benchmarking))
- **gene-cluster-family-model-distance-querying**: Use when pre-computed BiG-SLiCE GCF models exist (e.g., the T=900 run yielding 29,955 GCFs) and you need to quantify how close each model is to curated MIBiG v2.0 reference BGCs, or to validate feature-space separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-family-model-distance-querying))
- **gene-cluster-family-querying-against-centroid-models**: Use when you have one or more new antiSMASH-annotated BGCs and an existing BiG-SLiCE SQLite3 result database, and need to rank-match the new BGCs against precomputed GCF models instead of re-clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-family-querying-against-centroid-models))
- **gene-cluster-redundancy-clustering**: Use when you have antiSMASH/gutSMASH output directories for many genomes and need a nonredundant gene-cluster reference set (and optional GCF assignments) before mapping metagenomic/metatranscriptomic reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-cluster-redundancy-clustering))
- **gene-co-expression-correlation-analysis**: Use when a gene-expression or feature table (e.g., GTEx Gene-Tissue Expression data) needs to be queried for which features correlate with one prime feature, with coefficients and p-values for each. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-co-expression-correlation-analysis))
- **gene-co-expression-correlation-computation**: Use when two FPKM-normalized expression matrices (rows=gene symbols, columns=sample ids) for two physiological conditions are available and differential co-expression edges between gene pairs must be quantified and significance-tested. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-co-expression-correlation-computation))
- **gene-co-expression-network-construction**: Use when you have a bulk RNA-seq count matrix (e.g., 38 3xTg-AD/WT female hippocampus samples across three ages) and need to identify co-expression modules correlated with traits such as genotype or age. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-co-expression-network-construction))
- **gene-co-expression-network-inference**: Use when raw gene expression data (e.g. breast or prostate cancer expression matrices) must be turned into a weighted gene co-expression network and the resulting GCN's performance needs to be assessed via overlap, topological, and biological criteria. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-co-expression-network-inference--69fd3b1264c002e1))
- **gene-co-expression-network-inference**: Use when you have a raw UMI count matrix from a defined cell population (e.g. dysfunctional CD8+ T cells from MARS-seq melanoma, GSE123139) and want gene-level co-expression edges that recapitulate known functional annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-co-expression-network-inference--94fd571f2d03b6e1))
- **gene-co-expression-network-pruning**: Use when edge-weighted gene co-expression adjacency matrices (normal/cancer conditions per cancer type) are too dense for downstream fusion or community detection and must be pruned to their backbone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-co-expression-network-pruning))
- **gene-co-expression-robustness-half-sampling**: Use when comparing gene network inference methods (e.g., scLink, PIDC, GENIE3) for edge-stability across cell subsamples and for wall-clock runtime/memory on matched scRNA-seq inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-co-expression-robustness-half-sampling))
- **gene-coexpression-network-degree-analysis**: Use when you have constructed gene co-expression (or differential co-expression) networks and need to validate their topology as approximately scale-free before downstream hub/driver-node analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-coexpression-network-degree-analysis))
- **gene-coexpression-network-estimation**: Use when you need topology-based pathway enrichment on gene expression data (e.g. GEO breast cancer or TCGA prostate cancer matrices) and must first estimate weighted, directed adjacency matrices per pathway from curated interaction databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-coexpression-network-estimation))
- **gene-coexpression-network-hub-derivation**: Use when deriving cell-type-specific senescence (or age-dynamic) gene signatures from aggregated single-cell RNA-seq spanning multiple tissues and ages, requiring weighted hub gene lists rather than simple marker lists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-coexpression-network-hub-derivation))
- **gene-coexpression-network-inference**: Use when a time-series abundance table (taxon-by-sample) should be partitioned into correlated subnetworks/modules whose membership reflects seasonal or temporal community structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/gene-coexpression-network-inference))
- **gene-coexpression-network-inference**: Use when filtered RNA-seq expression data from stress conditions (e.g., soybean tissues infected with C. gregata, F. graminearum, F. virguliforme, M. phaseolina, P. pachyrhizi) must be converted into a coexpression network for downstream gene-module and candidate-gene mining. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-coexpression-network-inference))
- **gene-community-modularity-detection**: Use when you have a fused gene co-expression network (e.g., Gephi-format .gexf from the GeneNetFusion pipeline) and need to partition genes into communities for biomarker extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-community-modularity-detection))
- **gene-content-prediction-accuracy-assessment**: Use when validating phylogeny-based gene-family prediction accuracy on genomes with known annotations, e.g. measuring per-genome and per-functional-category accuracy of PICRUSt predictions from a KO/COG reference table and a 16S marker-gene tree. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-content-prediction-accuracy-assessment))
- **gene-coordinates**: Convert genomic intervals between coordinate conventions, normalise/compare variant representations, and detect assembly or contig-naming mismatches before they corrupt an analysis. Use whenever coordinates cross a format, tool, or assembly boundary: BED/GFF/GTF/VCF/SAM/BAM/region-string conversion, 0-based half-open vs 1-based inclusive, left-aligning/trimming indels, checking whether two variant records describe the same change, GRCh37 vs hg19 vs GRCh38 vs T2T, chr-prefix mismatch, or liftover. Triggers include 'off by one', '0-based', '1-based', 'coordinate system', 'normalize variant', 'w… ([oscar-wang-xin/my-skills](https://github.com/oscar-wang-xin/my-skills/tree/HEAD/gene-coordinates))
- **gene-copy-number-correction**: Use when amplicon (16S/18S rRNA) or microarray profiles in biom, QIIME, GAAS, Unifrac, or generic tabular format with read counts and unaltered Greengenes taxonomic assignments must be corrected for GCN bias, or when qPCR total abundances need correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-copy-number-correction))
- **gene-copy-number-tree-annotation**: Use when you have GCN trait estimates for Greengenes OTUs/taxa plus the Greengenes phylogeny or taxonomy, and need to quantify how strongly GCN tracks phylogeny (e.g. Pagel's lambda) before inferring GCN for unsequenced lineages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-copy-number-tree-annotation))
- **gene-count-frequency-summarization**: Use when scorecard single-quadrant analyses have been run for multiple comparisons (e.g., VAN/CPT/DEL/LZD vs untreated in S. aureus dataset 1) and you need a tabulated count of genes per quadrant per comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-count-frequency-summarization))
- **gene-count-matrix-merging**: Use when you hold one two-column gene abundance file per sample (gene identifier + raw count, or gene identifier + FPKM) and need a single merged matrix to feed differential expression tools like DESeq2Shiny. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-count-matrix-merging))
- **gene-count-qc-verification**: Use when reproducing or validating gene catalog construction counts — e.g. after running MetaGeneMark CDS prediction or after BLASTP/Jaccard de-redundification — to check totals and per-source partitions against published VIRGO values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-count-qc-verification))
- **gene-count-significance-dot-encoding**: Use when you have a pathway or feature summary table with activation z-scores (fold-changes), p-values, and gene counts across conditions (e.g., brain regions and timepoints) and need a single composite heatmap visualizing all three modalities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-count-significance-dot-encoding))
- **gene-coverage-calculation**: Use when after calling RNA modifications with Dogme/modkit across replicates, you need to know what fraction of annotated genes contain shared modification sites per modification type (m6A, m5C, inosine, pseudouridine, Nm). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-coverage-calculation))
- **gene-coverage-quality-assessment**: Use when comparing bin2cell-reconstructed cell objects against default 8 μm Visium HD bins and you need a quantitative per-object quality signal such as per-cell gene count coverage or CellTypist prediction confidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-coverage-quality-assessment))
- **gene-curation**: Perform a ClinGen gene–disease clinical validity curation end to end: organize source papers, make a lumping/splitting decision, score genetic (case-level + segregation) and experimental evidence against the built-in ClinGen SOP v10.1 rules (no SOP docs needed), look up HPO/MONDO terms, and produce GCI-ready copy-paste sheets, an evidence-summary, and a presentation deck. Use when the user mentions ClinGen, a GCEP, gene-disease validity, gene curation, scoring probands/variants, or the Gene Curation Interface (GCI). Works for any gene/disease, not just the example (STAMBP / MIC-CAP). ([chen-dawn/clingen-gene-curation-skill](https://github.com/chen-dawn/clingen-gene-curation-skill/tree/HEAD/gene-curation))
- **gene-database**: Query NCBI Gene via E-utilities/Datasets API. ([artubss/SKILLS-CLAUDE-CODE](https://github.com/artubss/SKILLS-CLAUDE-CODE/tree/HEAD/skills/skills/scientific/gene-database) · ★12)
- **gene-database**: Archived provider note for gene lookup. Use bioinformatics-database-retrieval for active project routing. ([YuukiAS/AI_Skills_Collection](https://github.com/YuukiAS/AI_Skills_Collection/tree/HEAD/skills/domains/bioinformatics/gene-database) · ★2)
- **gene-database**: Query NCBI Gene via E-utilities for curated gene records across 1M+ taxa. Retrieve official gene symbols, aliases, RefSeq accessions, summary descriptions, genomic coordinates, GO annotations, and interaction data. Use for gene ID resolution, cross-species queries, and gene function summaries. For sequence retrieval use Ensembl; for expression data use geo-database. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-database) · ★1)
- **gene-database**: 通过E-utilities/Datasets API查询NCBI基因数据库。按基因符号/ID搜索，获取基因信息(RefSeqs、GO、位置、表型)，批量查找，用于基因注释和功能分析。 ([Victory-Hugo/S2-Agent-Skill](https://github.com/Victory-Hugo/S2-Agent-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/code/databases/gene-database) · ★1)
- **gene-database-inclusion-filtering**: Use when combining multiple reference gene collections (e.g. ResFinder, CARD, MegaRes, AMRFinderPlus, ARGANNOT, ResFinderFG) into one non-redundant database and you need reproducible source-specific inclusion filters plus identity/coverage-based clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-database-inclusion-filtering))
- **gene-database-label-extraction**: Use when a list of candidate HVGs (e.g. from FindVariableFeatures(), variance, scannpy, M3drop) must be classified as communication-related or not, using database-derived gene tags as the labeling criterion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-database-label-extraction))
- **gene-db**: Query NCBI Gene via E-utilities/Datasets API. Search by symbol/ID, retrieve gene info (RefSeqs, GO, locations, phenotypes), batch lookups, for gene annotation and functional analysis. ([LUNARTECH-X/superpowers](https://github.com/LUNARTECH-X/superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/academy-skills/gene-db) · ★17)
- **gene-degree-ranking-baseline-comparison**: Use when you must reproduce or benchmark an ablation of a network driver-gene method against degree-only and unweighted baselines, scoring each list against a curated benchmark like the 616-gene CGC set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-degree-ranking-baseline-comparison))
- **gene-deletion-class-prediction**: Use when you have a space-delimited expression file (N+1 rows, 3m columns: eD1-eDm, eS1-eSm, eL1-eLm) for gene deletion events and need to classify each event as 'Redundant' or 'Unique'. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-deletion-class-prediction))
- **gene-detection-threshold-filtering**: Use when a raw digital gene expression matrix (e.g., Cell Ranger output UMI counts) has not been filtered or normalized, signaled by indication='none' in scTITANS_full, and a cleaned, normalized matrix is needed before trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-detection-threshold-filtering))
- **gene-disease-association-annotation**: Use when a Cytoscape network of genes/proteins (e.g. built with a stringApp Disease Query such as query='Rett syndrome', cutoff=0.4, limit=20) needs disease-gene and compound-target annotations added from CyTargetLinker XGMML linksets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-disease-association-annotation))
- **gene-disease-association-data-ingestion**: Use when building a local, offline-queryable gene/phenotype-disease association knowledge base from RDF sources (DisGeNET-RDF, HOOM, SIO) for tools like VIBE that map HPO terms to ranked candidate genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-disease-association-data-ingestion))
- **gene-disease-association-intersection**: Use when a list of differentially expressed genes (e.g. sex-consistent F1_M1 / F-1_M-1 states from an 8-week exercise time point) must be prioritized against curated gene-disease associations such as Open Targets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-disease-association-intersection))
- **gene-disease-association-mapping**: Use when the goal is to confirm which genes are shared across a defined panel of intestine-related diseases, or to map specific genes (e.g. STK11, SMAD4/BMPR1A) to named syndromes using GutUDB's disease-gene association data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-disease-association-mapping))
- **gene-disease-association-scoring**: Use when patient symptoms encoded as HPO codes (e.g. HP:0002996) must be converted into a ranked candidate gene list backed by literature/curated gene-disease associations, e.g. as a module in a genome diagnostics pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-disease-association-scoring))
- **gene-domain-co-occurrence-analysis**: Use when you have a collection of BGCs tokenised as Pfam domain / subPfam combinations, have filtered them for redundancy using domain-based similarity networks, and need to discover latent gene sub-clusters that group together genes with correlated domain compositions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gene-domain-co-occurrence-analysis))
- **gene-dosage-sensitivity-scoring**: Use when annotating genes with binary LOF/GOF dosage-sensitivity scores for SV pathogenicity prediction, especially when sex-chromosome genes lack ClinGen TS scores and need an HI-based proxy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-dosage-sensitivity-scoring))
- **gene-dropout-probability-thresholding**: Use when a raw gene-by-cell count matrix (csv) from scRNA-seq contains dropout zeros — genes observed at moderate expression in some cells but undetected in others — and only high-confidence dropouts should be imputed without altering unaffected values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-dropout-probability-thresholding))
- **gene-drug-automd**: Plan and run gene/protein/PDB/mmCIF/AlphaFold 3 or nucleic-acid target workflows with small molecules using AutoDock Vina and GROMACS, including protocol recommendation, runtime estimates, system building, MD analysis, reports, and standalone Python reproduction scripts for EM/NVT/NPT/production/resume/analysis. Keep each study in a portable projects/project-name directory, parallel to the Skill by default or under a user-selected root, with absolute input/config/output paths and the required doctor, plan, run sequence. Classify missing or risky inputs as BLOCKER, CONFIRM or WARNING with corr… ([a1665779280-cyber/GeneDrug-AutoMD-Skill](https://github.com/a1665779280-cyber/GeneDrug-AutoMD-Skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★23)
- **gene-drug-interaction-mapping**: Use when a trait- or cell-type-associated co-expression module or GRN (e.g. the M11 network with TFs GATA2, MAFF, FOSB) has already been inferred and needs to be linked to repurposable drugs through drug-target interaction databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-drug-interaction-mapping))
- **gene-embedding-cosine-neighbor-ranking**: Use when a trained GeneVector embedding (genes.vec) is available and you must rank a query gene's nearest neighbors by cosine similarity to confirm pathway co-membership or seed a metagene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-embedding-cosine-neighbor-ranking))
- **gene-embedding-entropy-ranking**: Use when trained graph-embedding outputs (e.g., DGMP logits and ZF, Zin, Zout, ZMLP vectors) are available and you need to isolate genes with dissimilar neighbors to diagnose embedding class separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-embedding-entropy-ranking))
- **gene-embedding-learning**: Use when constructing cell-lineage-specific gene embeddings from scRNA-seq data and a prior gene interaction network, e.g. inputs to downstream driver-regulator or RGM analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-embedding-learning))
