- **gene-enrichment**: Perform comprehensive gene enrichment and pathway analysis using gseapy (ORA and GSEA), PANTHER, STRING, Reactome, and 40+ MedToolkit tools. Supports GO enrichment (BP, MF, CC), KEGG, Reactome, WikiPathways, MSigDB Hallmark, and 220+ Enrichr libraries. Handles multiple ID types (gene symbols, Ensembl, Entrez, UniProt), multiple organisms (human, mouse, rat, fly, worm, yeast), customizable backgrounds, and multiple testing correction (BH, Bonferroni). Use when users ask about gene enrichment, pathway analysis, GO term enrichment, KEGG pathway analysis, GSEA, over-representation analysis, funct… ([rbr7/MedClawMini](https://github.com/rbr7/MedClawMini/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-enrichment))
- **gene-environment-module-annotation**: Use when you have a list of differentially expressed human genes (from microarray, bulk RNA-seq, or a clustered Seurat single-cell object) and want to test which curated environmental gene modules (infection, diet, cigarette smoking, toxic chemicals) are statistically over-represented. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-environment-module-annotation))
- **gene-enzyme-annotation-retrieval**: Use when a MetENP analysis has already produced significant metabolites mapped to KEGG compound IDs and you need the downstream reaction, gene, and enzyme annotations for those metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-enzyme-annotation-retrieval))
- **gene-essentiality**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Systems_Biology/systems-biology/gene-essentiality) · ★7)
- **gene-essentiality**: Query, interpret, and integrate DepMap (Cancer Dependency Map) CRISPR knockout essentiality scores — including CERES, Chronos, gene-effect inversion, pan-essential vs selective-essential separation, and integration with expression / mutation / proteomics. Use when you need to know whether a gene is a dependency in a tumour type, find selective vulnerabilities, or correlate essentiality with another omics layer. ([UViiVe/fleet-skills-public](https://github.com/UViiVe/fleet-skills-public/tree/HEAD/gene-essentiality))
- **gene-essentiality-annotation**: Use when you have a list of essential genes from single-gene deletion analysis of a metabolic reconstruction and want to map approved drugs onto those targets to prioritize repurposing hypotheses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-essentiality-annotation))
- **gene-expression-analysis**: Take a public NCBI GEO series (GSE...), preserve it, verify its identities, and compute a two-group differential expression (Welch t-test per probe, Benjamini-Hochberg; not limma) with the platform's tools, never by hand. ([anonymous-temp/EviMed-Science](https://github.com/anonymous-temp/EviMed-Science/tree/HEAD/OpenScience/capabilities/gene-expression-analysis) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **gene-expression-and-accessibility-factor-visualization**: Use when a MuData object with paired scRNA-seq and scATAC-seq modalities already contains a fitted latent factor space (e.g. X_mofa in obsm) and you need to interpret what each factor represents biologically. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-expression-and-accessibility-factor-visualization))
- **gene-expression-based-network-model-annotation**: Use when a validated biomolecular regulatory network model must be personalized with normalized RNA-seq expression values before running dynamical network analyses of cell fate or treatment response. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-expression-based-network-model-annotation))
- **gene-expression-baseline-trajectory-comparison**: Use when benchmarking whether incorporating VDJ features into trajectory inference yields higher fate-prediction accuracy than expression-only trajectory analysis, e.g. on fetal immune atlas DP-to-CD4/CD8 T-cell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-baseline-trajectory-comparison))
- **gene-expression-biclustering**: Use when the input is a continuous (or discretized) gene expression matrix and the goal is to discover overlapping trend-preserving (order-preserving) biclusters and compare them against planted ground truth or other algorithms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-biclustering))
- **gene-expression-binarization**: Use when preprocessing sparse single-cell RNA-seq matrices (e.g. Xin GSE81608) to build a binary low-dimensional representation of cells, where dropouts must be handled as signals rather than imputed away. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-binarization))
- **gene-expression-binning**: Use when you have null (e.g., gRNA-free or split-group) P values from single-cell DE or co-expression testing and need to check P-value uniformity across expression levels via expression-stratified KS tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-binning))
- **gene-expression-binning-from-bulk-replicates**: Use when matched bulk RNA-seq replicates and a single-cell count matrix are available and you need to partition genes by bulk expression level (true-zero silent genes vs. expressed bins) to test whether an imputation method retains biological zeros while imputing dropouts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-binning-from-bulk-replicates))
- **gene-expression-clinical-variable-correlation**: Use when whole-blood gene expression measurements and matched per-sample clinical laboratory values (e.g. CRP, lymphocyte counts, IFN scores) are available and you need to test whether gene abundance is linearly associated with disease-severity markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-clinical-variable-correlation))
- **gene-expression-clustering**: Use when a CSV gene-expression data frame with gene identifiers and log-fold-change (LFC) values must be partitioned into distinct expression-pattern clusters with per-gene membership probabilities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-clustering))
- **gene-expression-clustering-and-go-enrichment**: Use when a Lamian lamian_test run has produced significant TDE or XDE genes (FDR.overall &lt; 0.05) and you need to organize them into expression-dynamics clusters and interpret each cluster via GO enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-clustering-and-go-enrichment))
- **gene-expression-cohort-aggregation**: Use when building a subtype reference for PAM50-style classification from multiple subtype-labeled RNA-seq cohorts and the goal is a cohort-composition-independent centroid table (e.g. matching Supplementary Material Table S3). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-cohort-aggregation))
- **gene-expression-constraint-integration**: Use when when you have a generic constraint-based metabolic model, RNA-seq or transcriptomics data (FPKM or similar), and you want to reduce the underdetermination of metabolic flux predictions by encoding which reactions are expected to be active or inactive based on their constituent enzymes'. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-constraint-integration))
- **gene-expression-contour-overlay-on-tsne**: Use when a pooled t-SNE embedding of scRNAseq clusters needs annotation with expression contours of marker genes (e.g. Cd4, Cd8a) to identify compartment or lineage identity of clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-contour-overlay-on-tsne))
- **gene-expression-correlation-analysis**: Use when a bulk expression matrix (e.g. TCGA AML) and a subclone-associated gene list from scRNA-seq (e.g. GATA2R361C subclone) must be linked to test whether a marker gene like VIM defines a co-expressed regulon. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-correlation-analysis--5ef21c747df4b5a4))
- **gene-expression-correlation-analysis**: Use when comparing gene-gene correlation landscapes between an original measurement domain and a cross-modality mapping (e.g., scMMGAN spatial output), to flag spurious correlations and surface novel candidate associations like SLC2A1–P2RX7. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-correlation-analysis--d84e911d7dbd0df7))
- **gene-expression-correlation-analysis**: Use when a normalized single-cell expression matrix (imported from H5AD into Cellenium's PostgreSQL backend) is loaded for one study and you need to find all genes whose expression is highly correlated to a query gene, transcriptome-wide, within that study. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-correlation-analysis--105c86e5c023d878))
- **gene-expression-correlation-evaluation**: Use when two gene-matching approaches (e.g. protein-embedding distance and BLAST hits) disagree for a set of ortholog candidates and raw count scRNA-seq data is available to score candidates by expression correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-correlation-evaluation))
- **gene-expression-csv-formatting**: Use when preparing cross-platform transcriptome data (e.g., GEO series such as GSE151189 or GSE59098) for ingestion by Ciclops, whose --train_path/--valid_path inputs must be .csv files with sample names as the first column and labels as the last column. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-csv-formatting))
- **gene-expression-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw TCGA-style FPKM gene expression matrices must be reduced to actively expressed genes before co-expression network construction, to remove background/noisy genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-data-preprocessing--54f0a30f607d04c8))
- **gene-expression-data-preprocessing**: Use when a raw expression matrix from NGS/microarray experiments (e.g., TCGA, GTEx-style gene-by-sample data frames) shows PCR artifacts, heteroskedasticity in high-signal features, or non-normal/multimodal feature distributions before correlation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-data-preprocessing--0c00a62e5919821a))
- **gene-expression-data-separation-and-granulation**: Use when input is a continuous gene-expression microarray or RNA-seq matrix to be clustered for trend-preserving (order-preserving) biclusters, and many genes are inactive under some conditions, so raw-value biclustering would be distorted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-data-separation-and-granulation))
- **gene-expression-data-simulation**: Use when you need to benchmark a statistical mediation test or prioritize candidate driver genes: simulate gene expression with known mediated vs. independent cis-trans pairs before running the test. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-data-simulation))
- **gene-expression-dataframe-normalization**: Use when a raw gene-expression matrix (e.g., tab-separated GSE-style text or CSV loaded as a Pandas DataFrame) needs an internal reference gene identified and single-gene normalization to reduce between-group sample variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-dataframe-normalization))
- **gene-expression-dataset-benchmarking**: Use when you need to compare pathway enrichment methods on human disease gene expression datasets by measuring how highly each method ranks known target disease pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-expression-dataset-benchmarking))
- **gene-expression-dataset-loading**: Reference for retrieving the exact expression datasets (type 2 diabetes from the Whitehead deposit and airway epithelia from AGED) needed to reproduce PLAGE pathway-activity analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-expression-dataset-loading))
- **gene-expression-decoder-implementation**: Use when re-implementing or extending the scGAE decoder stack: you have a preprocessed scRNA-seq count matrix plus a KNN cell-graph adjacency matrix and need a single autoencoder that reconstructs both X and A from a shared latent embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-decoder-implementation))
- **gene-expression-dissimilarity-matrix-computation**: Use when connecting an scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. zebrafish GSE66688/GSE112294 or Drosophila dge_normalized.txt) to spatial gene measurements and you must supply cost_matrix, sc_dmat, and is_dmat to SpaOTsc. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-dissimilarity-matrix-computation))
- **gene-expression-distribution-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have per-gene MRSD scores (*.results.mrsd.txt) plus TPM values for the same genes and want to test whether expression level alone predicts required sequencing depth or full-transcript coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-distribution-overlap-analysis))
- **gene-expression-feature-classification**: Use when the goal is to predict evolutionary targets of gene deletion events ('Redundant' vs 'Unique' classes) from two-species expression data, or to benchmark classifier power across four added-noise SD levels (0.001, 0.01, 0.1, 1). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-feature-classification))
- **gene-expression-feature-ranking**: Use when you need to rank/ prioritize genes from a labeled high-dimensional gene-expression matrix (e.g., TCGA RNA-seq) as biomarker candidates, instead of traditional DEG analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-feature-ranking))
- **gene-expression-feature-subsetting**: Use when the scRNA-seq dataset is too large (e.g. &gt;1 million cells) or resource-limited to train a feature-selection model on all genes at once, and you need an importance-ranked feature set before final classifier training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-feature-subsetting))
- **gene-expression-filter-based-feature-selection**: Use when training mLR subtype classifiers on whole-transcriptome RNA-seq matrices where a reduced, informative gene space is needed and filter scores or limma DE statistics can define the feature set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-filter-based-feature-selection))
- **gene-expression-filtering**: Use when raw gene-by-cell UMI count matrices from multiple individuals (e.g., human PBMC, mouse lung, human skin 10x Chromium data) must be filtered of lowly-expressed genes and reduced to a top-1000-HVG feature space before clustering or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-filtering))
- **gene-expression-filtering-of-network-priors**: Use when you have a motif- and TSS-derived candidate TF–target mask built from ATAC-seq peaks and need to restrict it to genes detectably expressed in your scRNA-seq dataset prior to EM-based GRN inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-filtering-of-network-priors))
- **gene-expression-gradient-visualization**: Use when you have cross-modality latent embeddings (e.g. from scConfluence's diagonal integration of morphology and scRNA-seq) and need to inspect whether a marker gene such as Fezf2 shows a graded pattern along a biological manifold (e.g. apical-dendrite organization of IT neurons). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-gradient-visualization))
- **gene-expression-grid-image-generation**: Use when working with Visium HD 2 um bin data and you need to recover cells whose nuclei were not detected on the morphology image (e.g., nuclei absent from the tissue slice or with unusual shapes) by segmenting total gene expression rendered on the array grid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-expression-grid-image-generation))
- **gene-expression-heatmap-generation-by-response-class**: Use when you have SAM/samr significance-ranked gene lists and a quantile-normalized expression matrix from microarray data and need a dendrogram heatmap to check sample clustering by biological class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-heatmap-generation-by-response-class))
- **gene-expression-heatmap-visualization**: Use when a gene expression or omics matrix (e.g. heatmap_test.csv) with a matching sample-annotation file (e.g. group_info.csv) must be rendered as a clustered, group-annotated heatmap figure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-expression-heatmap-visualization))
- **gene-expression-heritability-estimation**: Use when standardizing cross-species (rat-to-human) differential expression effect sizes against GTEx v8 per-gene, per-tissue expression variability and its genetic component via h_SNP^2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-expression-heritability-estimation))
- **gene-expression-imputation-from-accessibility**: Use when you have a scATAC-seq snap object plus matched scRNA-seq expression data and need to link distal enhancer peaks to their putative target genes, or to rank enhancer–gene associations for benchmarking against eQTLs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-imputation-from-accessibility))
- **gene-expression-linear-modeling**: Use when you have normalized or voom-transformed gene expression counts/intensities indexed by gene and sample, along with an experimental design matrix specifying condition, batch, or covariate assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-expression-linear-modeling))
- **gene-expression-log-transformation**: Use when after normalizing total UMI counts per cell using normalize_total, and before PCA or feature selection. Use this when working with raw or depth-normalized count matrices where gene expression values span multiple orders of magnitude and variance is not homogeneous across expression levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-expression-log-transformation))
- **gene-expression-log2-normalization**: Use when a gene expression matrix (rows = human gene symbols, columns = samples) is about to be fed into DCNet or a deconvolution baseline, and raw counts/TPM values are not yet log-scaled. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-log2-normalization))
- **gene-expression-matrix-export**: Use when a cleaned, batch-corrected gene-by-sample matrix of the MDI-induced 3T3-L1 microarray data must be written to disk for downstream differential expression or gene set enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-export))
- **gene-expression-matrix-factorization**: Use when bulk, single-cell, or spatial RNA-Seq expression matrices need unsupervised decomposition into interpretable programs, or when cohorts with mismatched features/samples must be integrated without subsetting to a shared gene set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-factorization))
- **gene-expression-matrix-filtering**: Use when a raw or quantile-normalized microarray matrix (e.g. the PIMENTo example_unnorm.txt tab-separated file) contains a large low-intensity background population that must be separated from signal before differential analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-filtering))
- **gene-expression-matrix-imputation**: Use when preparing .csv gene expression matrices (e.g., GSE151189 training and GSE59098 validation) with missing values for cross-platform model training in Ciclops, where train and test data must share aligned gene distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-imputation))
- **gene-expression-matrix-mean-centering**: Use when compiling multiple NCBI GEO series into one expression matrix and you need to remove per-experiment offsets so values from different GSE studies are comparable before joining or downstream correlation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-mean-centering))
- **gene-expression-matrix-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., GSE65525 Buettner, GSE74547 Kolodziejczyk, GSE75748 Chu-time) must be normalized before c-CSN/CNDM construction or before clustering comparisons against GEM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-normalization))
- **gene-expression-matrix-normalization**: Use when when you have raw or unnormalized gene expression data from microarray experiments (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-expression-matrix-normalization))
- **gene-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq expression matrix (genes x cells, count- or FPKM-like values) needs cleaning before dropout analysis, imputation, or clustering, and value range/gene sparsity must be normalized first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-preprocessing--842ea03072bbe7c2))
- **gene-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when the input is raw or non-conforming bulk RNA-seq expression data that must be reshaped into a gene (symbol) x sample matrix before reaction activity scoring or flux analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-preprocessing--f6aa12e7d4531266))
- **gene-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when building gene expression inputs for the GSFM FM active matrix: raw tumor/normal matrices (TCGA, GEO) and LINCS drug-induced profiles must be sample-filtered for cell-line correlation before STEP 2 transformation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-preprocessing--4c6740b1d028a4aa))
- **gene-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when a raw numeric expression matrix (rows = features/genes, columns = samples) contains missing values, outliers, or low-variance rows and must be prepared for feature selection and subgroup classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-processing))
- **gene-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when perturbation gene-expression series (e.g., ArrayExpress submissions) must be normalized, QC'd, assembled into an expression matrix, and converted to z-scores for pathway-activity modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-matrix-processing))
- **gene-expression-median-stratification**: Use when you have per-sample gene expression values (e.g., TCGA RNA data) matched to clinical survival metadata and need binary high/low expression groups for log-rank/KM prognosis testing of an eccDNA gene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-median-stratification))
- **gene-expression-memory-based-lineage-prediction**: Use when scRNA-seq data lacks lineage information and you need to infer small cell lineages, or when validating GEMLI lineage predictions against barcoding ground truth (e.g., GSE99915 MEF data) with precision/sensitivity/FPR metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-memory-based-lineage-prediction))
- **gene-expression-memory-validation**: Use when a normalized scRNA-seq matrix with barcode lineage annotations is available and you need to quantify whether lineage-related cells are transcriptomically more similar than random pairs before trusting lineage predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-memory-validation))
- **gene-expression-methylation-integration**: Use when integrating matched transcriptomic and epigenomic (methylation) p-value data to score genes, detect dysregulated network modules, or compare module-detection methods such as SMITE and FEM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-methylation-integration))
- **gene-expression-network-structure-learning**: Use when you have a directed reference graph (e.g., the 32-node/47-edge ALS KEGG graph or KEGG/Reactome interactomes) plus an RNA-seq expression matrix, and need to recover or convert it into a valid directed spanning tree (arborescence). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-network-structure-learning))
- **gene-expression-normalization-centering-scaling**: Use when a gene expression matrix (e.g. GEO/ArrayExpress pre-clinical CRC datasets, TCGA pCRC, organoid or PDX matrices) is to be classified with CMScaller or its NTP algorithm, since input must be centered and scaled before prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-normalization-centering-scaling))
- **gene-expression-permutation-null-generation**: Use when a scDEED-style reliability analysis needs a null distribution of cell-embedding similarity scores to classify 2D t-SNE/UMAP embeddings as dubious or trustworthy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-permutation-null-generation))
- **gene-expression-phenotype-simulation**: Use when you need controlled simulation benchmarks for TWAS training methods: you have a real genotype panel and want to vary causal variant proportion and heritability to compare out-of-sample test R2 and TWAS power across methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-phenotype-simulation))
- **gene-expression-prediction-evaluation**: Use when a trained model predicts tumor gene expression from somatic alterations and you must quantify held-out test-set accuracy (predicted vs measured GEP) and compare against baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-prediction-evaluation))
- **gene-expression-preprocessing-and-imputation**: Use when a raw gene expression matrix (e.g. from golubEsets Bioconductor data) contains missing values, outlier or sub-1 expression values, or low-variance rows that would break consensus partitioning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-preprocessing-and-imputation))
- **gene-expression-profile-aggregation**: Use when a clustered scRNA-seq AnnData object (scanpyobj.h5ad) must be reduced to per-cluster average expression profiles for cell-type annotation or to match a cell line for LINCS L1000 deconvolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-profile-aggregation))
- **gene-expression-profile-z-score-normalization**: Use when preparing a Seurat-clustered scRNA-seq query whose top-N marker genes' average expression must be z-scored (e.g. via t(scale(t(...))) in R) before running deCS.correlation against a reference panel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-profile-z-score-normalization))
- **gene-expression-program-cell-type-specificity-assessment**: Use when you have a program usage matrix (e.g., cNMF usage values for k factors) plus known cell-type labels and need to count identity vs. shared-usage activity GEPs or compare methods on activity-program recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-program-cell-type-specificity-assessment))
- **gene-expression-program-estimation**: Use when a (genes x cells) log-normalized scRNAseq matrix must be decomposed into a small set of interpretable, nonnegative gene expression programs whose cell weights are regularized toward similarity of nearby cells in a q-diffused graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-program-estimation))
- **gene-expression-program-recovery-benchmarking**: Decision support for choosing between ICA- and NMF-based decomposition of scRNA-seq matrices, or for validating a program-recovery pipeline by matching recovered components to known embedded gene expression programs (GEPs). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-program-recovery-benchmarking))
- **gene-expression-quantification**: Use when raw RNA-seq reads have been mapped to a reference genome (BAM produced via ContextMap 2 and samtools) and per-gene read counts are needed as input for differential expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-quantification))
- **gene-expression-radar-visualization**: Use when you have a Seurat query object projected onto a reference TIL atlas and need to compare gene-expression profiles (e.g. exhaustion vs naive-like markers) across predicted cell states, or to verify subtype assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-radar-visualization))
- **gene-expression-ranking-preparation**: Use when you have a gene expression matrix (RNA-seq counts, microarray intensities, or normalized expression values) and need to perform gene set enrichment analysis on preranked gene lists. Use this skill when you want to rank genes by a univariate statistic (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-expression-ranking-preparation))
- **gene-expression-ranking-serialization**: Use when raw single-cell count matrices (e.g., HCL, GSE134355) must be converted into token sequences for a GPT-style autoregressive transformer that models gene-expression rankings rather than expression values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-ranking-serialization))
- **gene-expression-reconstruction-correlation-evaluation**: Use when a trained multimodal generative model (e.g. scMM on paired SHARE-seq scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq) has decoded predicted expression profiles that must be quantitatively scored against observed transcriptome counts and compared to a GAM baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-reconstruction-correlation-evaluation))
- **gene-expression-replacement-simulation**: Use when quantifying how much transcriptional identity (number of DE genes) a rare cell type needs before a detection method can still recover it, via controlled replacement of DE genes in subsampled scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-replacement-simulation))
- **gene-expression-repression-annotation**: Use when evaluating H3K27me3 (or other repressive-mark) ChIP-seq domain calls against functional ground truth: you have matched expression profiles for the same cell types and need per-cell-type sets of repressed genes to compute gene-body coverage fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-repression-annotation))
- **gene-expression-scaling**: Use when preparing a gene expression matrix (rows = genes, columns = cell populations) as input to ICELLNET interaction scoring, to bound per-gene influence before computing ligand–receptor and global communication scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-scaling))
- **gene-expression-signal-simulation**: Use when benchmarking pathway enrichment methods on simulated signals: you have a real expression matrix, need ground-truth dysregulated gene groups of varying sizes, and must measure empirical power and type-I error across replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-expression-signal-simulation))
- **gene-expression-similarity-matrix-computation**: Use when you have a normalized spatial transcriptomics expression matrix with spot coordinates and need multiple graph views (distinct similarity metrics) for multi-view graph autoencoder clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-expression-similarity-matrix-computation))
- **gene-expression-standardization-and-matrix-construction**: Use when expression data must be standardized per gene and pathway-restricted matrices built as input to an SVD-based pathway activity analysis such as PLAGE, or when reproducing metagene results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-expression-standardization-and-matrix-construction))
- **gene-expression-statistical-testing**: Use when you have fit a linear model to normalized gene expression data (microarray intensities or RNA-seq counts) and need to test for differential expression across experimental conditions or contrasts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-expression-statistical-testing))
- **gene-expression-stratification-plotting**: Use when you have scMMGAN-generated scRNA-seq profiles (output_fake2) from spatial transcriptomics input and need to test whether k=5 spectral k-means clusters or marker-gene strata (SLC2A1/CDK11A) retain the spatial organization of the tissue. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-stratification-plotting))
- **gene-expression-subgroup-clustering**: Use when a high-dimensional gene-expression matrix (e.g. TCGA GBM from gdc.cancer.gov/about-data/publications/gbm_exp) must be stratified into stable molecular subgroups and the effect of resampling count on consensus stability must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-subgroup-clustering))
- **gene-expression-subsetting-to-selected-cells**: Use when a user has defined a cell group during a CellexalVR VR session and downstream backend calculations (e.g. TF-TF correlation networks with propr rho) need the expression matrix restricted to exactly those selected cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-subsetting-to-selected-cells))
- **gene-expression-time-series-simulation**: Use when a forecasting or trajectory method needs ground-truth future expression states for benchmarking, by simulating noisy single-cell time-series data with BoolODE from random gene networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-time-series-simulation))
- **gene-expression-time-series-smoothing**: Use when you have a measured gene-expression time series (e.g. GSE6865, GDS38, GSE44415) that must be converted into continuous smooth curves prior to ODE model fitting or regulon scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-time-series-smoothing))
- **gene-expression-tpm-filtering-of-neoantigens**: Use when TSNAD v2.0 has produced DeepHLApan-scored candidate neoantigens from WES/WGS and matched tumor RNA-Seq is available, so that neoantigens from genes with TPM &lt; 1 must be removed before ranking high-confidence candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-tpm-filtering-of-neoantigens))
- **gene-expression-trajectory-visualization**: Use when a DESC-embedded single-cell dataset (e.g. 10,878 human monocytes across three processing batches) needs pseudotime ordering and per-batch visualization of a marker gene such as S100A8 along that trajectory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-trajectory-visualization))
- **gene-expression-z-score-normalization**: Use when raw perturbation-series microarray data must be normalized per series and converted to z-scores so expression changes from different perturbation experiments (e.g. ArrayExpress submissions) can be pooled for signature construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-z-score-normalization))
- **gene-expression-zscore-normalization**: Use when an FPKM/TPM expression matrix (genes x cells) from scRNA-Seq has been quality-filtered and expression-floored (expr.minimum = 0.01) and a scale-free gene profile is needed before correlation-distance-based cell clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-expression-zscore-normalization))
- **gene-family-abundance-inference**: Use when you have an ASV/OTU abundance table plus a FASTA of marker-gene study sequences and need inferred functional profiles (EC gene families, MetaCyc pathways) without shotgun metagenomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-family-abundance-inference))
- **gene-family-content-prediction**: Use when a workflow needs predicted gene family content for unsequenced taxa on a 16S marker gene reference tree, e.g. preparing the pre-calculated PICRUSt gene content file before metagenome inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-family-content-prediction))
- **gene-filtering-by-expression-level**: Use when you have a large gene expression matrix (e.g., thousands of genes) from normalized microarray or RNA-seq data and need to reduce computational burden before running pathway enrichment analysis (e.g., GSEA or GESECA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-filtering-by-expression-level))
- **gene-filtering-for-spatial-omics-pipelines**: Use when preprocessing spatial multi-omics data (e.g. spatial-CITE-seq with paired mRNA and protein modalities) for SMOPCA, and you must select spatially variable genes via SPARK before dimension reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-filtering-for-spatial-omics-pipelines))
- **gene-finding-translation**: Works with DNA sequence at the gene level, covering reverse complement, GC content and skew, k-mer (word) counts, translation under the standard genetic code, six-frame translation, and open reading frames, with the limits of calling a gene from sequence alone. Use for "translate this DNA", "find the ORFs", "what is the reverse complement", "what is the GC content", "which frame is this gene in", "count the codons", "is this a gene". Use sequence-formats for reading the file and coordinates, sequence-alignment to find genes by similarity to known ones, information-theory-sequences for entropy… ([allamiro/tamir-agent-skills](https://github.com/allamiro/tamir-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/bioinformatics-skills/skills/gene-finding-translation))
- **gene-flow-mapping**: 基因流图谱绘制技能。从群体基因组数据中精确绘制基因流的历史和空间图谱，涵盖迁移-漂变平衡理论、性别偏向与地理梯度的非对称基因流、时空基因流定年与适应性基因流分析。 ([gqy20/cc_plugins](https://github.com/gqy20/cc_plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/hybrid-speciation-expert/skills/gene-flow-mapping) · ★2)
- **gene-for-gene**: Curate gene-for-gene and effector–host interactions (guard/decoy vs direct recognition, effector GO tagging, R-gene extensions, inverse gene-for-gene/NETS). Use when a paper reports an avirulence/effector gene recognised by a host resistance gene, or an effector acting on a host target. ([PHI-base/phi-weaver](https://github.com/PHI-base/phi-weaver/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-for-gene) · ★1)
- **gene-function-papers-skill**: Find a gene's own function papers in PubMed ((symbol OR aliases) AND function/role/regulation/mechanism in title or abstract), rank them (gene in title, then the screen's cell type, then primary research over reviews) and download open-access PDFs. Use in the literature step for each top program gene and each regulator, to learn what the gene is known to do. ([EngreitzLab/PerturbNMF](https://github.com/EngreitzLab/PerturbNMF/tree/HEAD/src/Stage3_Interpretation/C_Annotation/agent/gene-function-papers-skill) · ★11)
- **gene-functional-annotation**: Use when a ranked disease-vs-control gene list (e.g. top-100 or top-10 biomarkers from scaLR's eval_and_analysis_pipeline) must be checked for enrichment of known disease or neurological-disorder genes to validate biomarker discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-functional-annotation))
- **gene-functional-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have two overlapping gene sets (e.g. coDIU and DIU genes from acorde) plus isoform-level functional annotations, and need to identify features/GO terms enriched in one set over the other. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-functional-enrichment-analysis))
- **gene-fusion-and-exitron-detection**: Use when RNA-seq data must be mined for gene-fusion and exitron-splicing events as variant inputs to neoantigen prediction, beyond the SNV/indel sources most tools cover. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-fusion-and-exitron-detection))
- **gene-fusion-detection**: Use when tumor RNA-Seq FASTQ reads are supplied to TSNAD v2.0 and fusion-derived neoantigen candidates must be generated alongside WES/WGS-based SNV/INDEL neoantigens. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-fusion-detection))
- **gene-fusion-detection-benchmarking**: Use when comparing computational time of scFusion against bulk fusion detection methods (Arriba, STAR-Fusion, EricScript, FusionCatcher) on real Smart-Seq scRNA-seq datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-fusion-detection-benchmarking))
- **gene-fusion-detection-long-read**: Use when you have full-length PacBio Iso-seq FASTA/CCS reads and want to identify gene fusions, novel fusion isoforms, or benchmark a long-read fusion caller against SQANTI/JAFFAL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-fusion-detection-long-read))
- **gene-gene-association-network-construction**: Use when you need per-cell, direct gene-gene association networks from a scRNA-seq expression matrix, e.g. to remove CSN's indirect-association overestimation or to identify hub genes in differentiation modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-gene-association-network-construction))
- **gene-gene-correlation-analysis**: Use when you need to validate denoised/imputed single-cell expression by comparing gene-gene Pearson correlations before and after imputation, or to detect spurious marker expression in a specific cell population. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-gene-correlation-analysis))
- **gene-gene-correlation-matrix-analysis**: Use when building or analyzing a gene-gene correlation matrix from log-normalized scRNA-seq data to score genes, identify well-correlated marker genes, or derive feature sets for cell-type clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-gene-correlation-matrix-analysis))
- **gene-gene-correlation-preservation-in-simulation**: Use when a clustering benchmark needs a simulated scRNA-seq dataset that retains realistic gene-gene correlation structure and per-type marginals, with controlled group sizes including a rare cell type (&lt;5% of cells). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-gene-correlation-preservation-in-simulation))
- **gene-gene-interaction-curation**: Use when performing topology-based pathway enrichment and no pre-built pathway network is available: dataset gene lists (e.g. 2784 breast cancer genes) need gene-gene edges harvested from KEGG/Reactome and assembled into adjacency matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-gene-interaction-curation))
- **gene-gene-interaction-extraction**: Use when you have a DEGGs subnetworks object of class Deggs (e.g. from generate_subnetworks on TCGA-BRCA counts) and need the significant gene-gene pairs listed per subgroup as candidate features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-gene-interaction-extraction))
- **gene-genomic-coordinate-annotation-and-sorting**: Use when a filtered gene-by-cell count matrix (e.g. SCEVAN input after removing cells with &lt;200 genes and genes in &lt;1% of cells) lacks genomic coordinates or is not yet ordered by genomic position prior to CNV segmentation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-genomic-coordinate-annotation-and-sorting))
- **gene-gget**: Fast unified queries to 20+ bioinformatics databases via gget. Use for quick lookups: gene/transcript info, BLAST/BLAT, sequences, AlphaFold structures, enrichment, OpenTargets, COSMIC, CELLxGENE, viral sequences. Triggers include 'gget', 'gene info', 'Ensembl', 'BLAST', 'AlphaFold', 'enrichr', 'OpenTargets', 'COSMIC', 'cellxgene', 'query database'. ([oscar-wang-xin/my-skills](https://github.com/oscar-wang-xin/my-skills/tree/HEAD/gene-gget))
- **gene-grouped-10-fold-cross-validation**: Use when training or tuning a regression model (e.g., ε-SVR with RBF kernel) on variant-level ΔΨ data where multiple variants come from the same gene and naive random folds would leak information between train and validation splits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-grouped-10-fold-cross-validation))
- **gene-hit-tracking-across-experimental-comparisons**: Use when several pairwise Treatment-vs-Control RNA-seq comparisons (e.g., oxacillin, loratadine, Cmpd8 co-treatments vs Control in MRSA USA100) need screening for genes extreme in log2 fold-change and padj&lt;=0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-hit-tracking-across-experimental-comparisons))
- **gene-homology-mapping**: Use when preparing single-cell RNA-seq count matrices from two or more species for integration and you must decide which homologous gene sets (O2O vs in-paralog-including HE/SH) define the shared feature space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-homology-mapping))
- **gene-hpo**: Local Human Phenotype Ontology (HPO) database and AI knowledge-base integration. Use to build a local HPO store (ontology + gene/disease annotations) from official releases, query terms/hierarchy (is_a/part_of), map free-text clinical notes (Chinese or English) to standard HPO terms, and rank candidate genes/diseases from a patient phenotype set via semantic similarity (Resnik/SimGIC). Triggers include 'HPO', 'Human Phenotype Ontology', 'phenotype', '表型', '临床表型', 'NL to HPO', 'phenotype to gene', 'rare disease', '语义相似度', 'HP:000'. ([oscar-wang-xin/my-skills](https://github.com/oscar-wang-xin/my-skills/tree/HEAD/gene-hpo))
- **gene-id-mapping**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Transcriptomics/expression-matrix/gene-id-mapping) · ★7)
- **gene-identifier-alignment-across-samples**: Use when you have one tabular gene abundance file per sample (FPKM or count files with genes in the first column) and need a single merged csv matrix keyed on gene identifiers for downstream bulk RNA-seq analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-identifier-alignment-across-samples))
- **gene-identifier-deduplication-and-filtering**: Use when when loading gene expression data (e.g., from GEO via getGEO or microarray ExpressionSet objects) that contains duplicate rows mapped to the same Gene ID, missing gene identifiers, or identifiers encoded with placeholder strings ('///') that indicate failed or ambiguous annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-identifier-deduplication-and-filtering))
- **gene-identifier-format-standardization**: Use when when you have differential expression results from genes, miRNAs, proteins, or lipids with heterogeneous identifier formats (raw gene names, Ensembl accessions, RefSeq IDs, or gene symbols) and need to perform pathway enrichment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gene-identifier-format-standardization))
- **gene-identifier-key-joining**: Use when you hold multiple per-sample two-column gene count files (gene identifier, raw count) that must be combined into one gene x sample matrix before downstream RNA-seq analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-identifier-key-joining))
- **gene-identifier-mapping**: Use when feature names from CCLE omics layers (e.g. RNA splicing Ensemble IDs) must be converted to common gene names or Bigg IDs before matching against Recon3D reactions or SynLethDB SL gene pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-identifier-mapping--8720dbc8e7279d3c))
- **gene-identifier-mapping**: Use when pathway node identifiers retrieved from databases like KEGG or Reactome must be matched to the identifiers of your input gene set (e.g. ENTREZID to UNIPROT) before assembling edge lists for topology-based pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-identifier-mapping--8f4a5be0442d6010))
- **gene-identifier-mapping**: Use when a simulation or analysis step requires harmonising gene identifiers (e.g., symbols to Entrez) before constructing GO-annotation-based similarity networks or filtering genes by annotation content. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-identifier-mapping))
- **gene-identifier-mapping**: Use when an RNA-seq counts matrix carries Ensembl gene IDs but downstream GSVA, .gmt gene-set matching, TPM integration, or differential analysis in ARMT requires gene-symbol identifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-identifier-mapping--5c8ee806844728a1))
- **gene-identifier-mapping**: Use when you have a gene ID list associated with a biological process of interest and need to derive its PPIN adjacency edge list for downstream PPIN activity scoring in ORIGINS2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-identifier-mapping--022e8f34b15ddcdc))
- **gene-identifier-mapping-for-enrichment**: Use when BIT output rank tables (e.g., mm10_RUNX1_rank_table.csv) contain TR gene symbols but the downstream enrichment tool requires Entrez IDs, such as clusterProfiler::enrichGO. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-identifier-mapping-for-enrichment))
- **gene-identifier-mapping-for-rem-retrieval**: Use when you have a gene list (e.g. DE genes from Seurat FindAllMarkers) and need the REMs associated to those genes, with cell type/tissue-specific filtering via EpiRegio. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-identifier-mapping-for-rem-retrieval))
- **gene-identifier-standardization**: Use when raw CCLE/DepMap omics feature matrices mix identifier namespaces (e.g. RNA splicing ensemble IDs, BiggIDs) and feature names must be mapped to common gene names before cross-omics ML training or network output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-identifier-standardization))
- **gene-identifier-to-name-mapping**: Use when an IsoPops lrCaptureSeq catalog carries gene IDs only and transcripts must be attributed to gene names before per-gene isoform counts or per-experiment read totals can be tabulated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-identifier-to-name-mapping))
- **gene-importance-ranking**: Use when a trained Cancer-Finder checkpoint exists (e.g., ST model with 5000-gene input) and the goal is to explain malignant-vs-normal spot classification by extracting a ranked, per-gene importance list from spatial transcriptomics slides. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-importance-ranking))
- **gene-importance-stability-analysis**: Use when you need to verify that a trained SIMS/TabNet classifier's sparse gene-selection masks for a specific cell class (e.g., Pvalb+ interneurons) are consistent across independent runs, or to recover an interpretable marker-gene set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-importance-stability-analysis))
- **gene-index**: Use when building the gene index that gene-level annotations, cohort tables and rankings join onto (hvantk annotate spine) from the Ensembl gene-structure and HGNC datasets on a Slurm cluster: staging and building the two inputs, choosing the biotype, checking the gene count and the HGNC mapping rate, writing a receipt, and adding annotation axes onto it with hvantk annotate prepare and compose. Also when the gene index comes out empty, the mapping rate is far below what you expect, or a build stops with "HGNC join changed the row count" or "needs --hgnc". ([bigbio/hvantk](https://github.com/bigbio/hvantk/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-index) · ★6)
- **gene-influence-quantification-per-cluster**: Use when marker genes must be identified for clusters from pretrained deep learning embeddings (e.g., Miscell) and you need per-gene, per-cluster influence scores rather than t-test/Wilcoxon differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-influence-quantification-per-cluster))
- **gene-influence-score-computation**: Use when you have a CEFCON-constructed cell-lineage-specific GRN with learned attention coefficients and need to rank MFVS/MDS driver gene candidates by importance to output driver regulators. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-influence-score-computation))
- **gene-info**: Retrieves comprehensive gene information including PubMed publication counts, NCBI summaries, and Ensembl transcript data. Supports batch processing and file input. Invoke when the user asks for gene details, publication statistics, or needs to analyze a list of genes. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Evidence%20Insight/gene-info) · ★2.1k · 2 copies)
- **gene-interaction-network-subsetting**: Use when building a differential co-expression network from TCGA RNA-seq expression matrices and a prior gene-interaction network is available to restrict which gene-pair correlations are computed and tested. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-interaction-network-subsetting))
- **gene-interaction-table-export**: Use when a fitted PhenoPath model exists and you must rank genes by |covariate-pseudotime interaction effect|, label interactions of interest, and write the results as a per-gene CSV table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-interaction-table-export))
- **gene-keys**: Генные Ключи (Gene Keys, Ричард Радд) — Голограмма: 11 сфер, 4 Главных Дара, последовательности Венеры и Жемчужины, спектр Тень → Дар → Сиддхи, программные партнёры через gene_keys.py. Use when the user mentions Генные Ключи, Gene Keys, голограмма, активационная последовательность, ядерная рана, Сиддхи. ([aumkara/bazi-toolkit](https://github.com/aumkara/bazi-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-keys))
- **gene-kim**: Gene Kim's expertise — DevOps, Three Ways, Five Ideals, value streams, deployment pipelines, flow, and organizational transformation. ([getfatday/skills](https://github.com/getfatday/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/dream-team/templates/team-members/gene-kim/skills/gene-kim))
- **gene-knowledge-integration**: Given a gene symbol (e.g. TPMT), query 3 public databases (ClinGen CAR, PharmGKB, Monarch) to obtain gene registry info, FDA drug labels, clinical annotations, and gene-phenotype associations. Save all results into a JSON file. ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gene-knowledge-integration) · ★399 · 4 copies)
- **gene-length-annotation**: Use when building or extending the VIRGO non-redundant gene catalog and you need per-gene CDS predictions and gene lengths in bp from assembled metagenomic contigs or isolate genome sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-length-annotation))
- **gene-length-bias-quantification**: Use when validating that a simulated non-UMI scRNA-seq dataset recapitulates gene length bias, or when contrasting length-biased full-length protocols against UMI-based protocols that lack it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-length-bias-quantification))
- **gene-length-normalization**: Reference for computing per-gene abundances from read-to-gene alignment hits when reads may match multiple genes equally well, and when gene-length bias in shotgun metagenomic count data must be corrected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-length-normalization--9f03a684a8938e1c))
- **gene-length-normalization**: Use when a mapped gene frequency count table (reads per gene across samples) is the input and gene-level abundances must be compared across samples; long genes systematically accumulate more reads, biasing downstream normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-length-normalization--5b686642a7b48c75))
- **gene-length-normalization-mutation-bias-correction**: Use when computing per-gene mutational signature contribution (CCA) matrices from a MAF/VCF plus P and S matrices, and relative abundance η_s,g must be corrected for gene length before filtering gene–signature pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-length-normalization-mutation-bias-correction))
- **gene-length-offset-matrix-computation**: Use when you have transcript-level quantification (salmon, Sailfish, or kallisto output) summarized to gene level by tximport, and you observe or suspect differential isoform usage across your experimental conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-length-offset-matrix-computation))
- **gene-length-read-count-normalization**: Use when raw featureCounts gene-level read counts (TopHat2/hg19 alignments, TCGA or Prensner PCa cohorts) must be corrected for sum-of-exons gene length before FunHoP node aggregation or Voom differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-length-read-count-normalization))
- **gene-level-alignment-score-aggregation**: Use when chromatin state sequences of thousands of protein-coding genes must be compared across two sample groups (e.g. male vs female NIH Roadmap epigenomes) to produce per-gene groups of EpiAlign alignment scores and a ranked list of differentially aligned genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-level-alignment-score-aggregation))
- **gene-level-cnv-matrix-construction**: Use when a CaSpER run has produced final.objects from runCaSpER and you need gene-level amplification/deletion calls (e.g. to benchmark 69% TPR / 0.3% FPR gene-based deletion detection against a genotyping array gold standard). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-level-cnv-matrix-construction))
- **gene-level-count-summarization-from-transcript-estimates**: Use when Salmon quasi-mapping quant outputs (quant.sf files) from simulated FASTQ reads must be converted to gene-level counts before running DESeq2 between simulated disease and normal groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-level-count-summarization-from-transcript-estimates))
- **gene-level-differential-expression-along-lineages**: Use when a common multi-condition trajectory (slingshot sds) already exists and the goal is to find genes whose expression patterns differ between conditions along its lineages after topology, progression, and fate-selection tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-level-differential-expression-along-lineages))
- **gene-level-dispersion-metric-computation**: Use when benchmarking imputation methods on scRNA-seq data and you need a transcriptome-landscape preservation metric — e.g. comparing median gene-level normalized intra dispersion across raw and imputed matrices over multiple datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-level-dispersion-metric-computation))
- **gene-level-geometric-mean-summary-statistics**: Use when a gene/isoform has multiple measured per-read tail lengths (e.g. from PAIso-seq CCS reads) and a single per-gene length is needed for group-level comparison against an external binary classification such as detected vs not-detected proteins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-level-geometric-mean-summary-statistics))
- **gene-level-microdiversity-profiling**: Use when mapping metagenomic reads of a co-occurring strain (e.g. phage 482_10.ph) back to its own reference and you need per-gene coverage, SNP types, and dN/dS to test whether within-individual diversity overlaps between-individual variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-level-microdiversity-profiling))
- **gene-level-p-value-reproduction**: Use when validating DriverPower-exclusive non-coding driver candidates by testing whether genes linked to mutated candidate elements show differential expression after correcting for copy number (SCNA) and tissue type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-level-p-value-reproduction))
- **gene-level-pvalue-table-preparation**: Use when a downstream pareg regression call needs a gene-by-pvalue dataframe and you only have raw matched tumor/normal expression data (e.g. GSE62944 TCGA-BRCA) that still requires a differential expression test per gene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-level-pvalue-table-preparation))
- **gene-level-read-count-quantification**: Use when starting from raw RNA-seq FASTQ files of a patient cohort and per-gene read counts plus DE statistics (P value, regulation direction) are needed as downstream input for node-level pathway aggregation, e.g. FunHoP/KEGG network analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-level-read-count-quantification))
- **gene-level-summarization-from-transcripts**: Use when you have transcript-level quantification files (salmon quant.sf.gz, kallisto h5, or Sailfish output) and need to perform gene-level differential expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-level-summarization-from-transcripts))
- **gene-level-versus-functional-level-differential-analysis**: Decision support for choosing annotation level (gene vs KO/EC/reaction) when reporting differential abundance results from case-control metagenomic count data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-level-versus-functional-level-differential-analysis))
- **gene-level-volcano-and-ma-plot-generation**: Use when after completing differential expression analysis (edgeR, DESeq2, or RankProduct) on count matrices to visualize fold-changes versus p-values and to assess the relationship between average expression levels and log2 fold-changes for gene-level omics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gene-level-volcano-and-ma-plot-generation))
- **gene-lipid-class-association**: Use when a lipid feature abundance matrix plus phenotype file must be linked to lipid classes (via genes) and analysed for differential lipid class, total chain length and hydrocarbon-chain saturation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-lipid-class-association))
- **gene-list-curator**: Curate literature-backed gene or protein lists for a requested function, pathway, molecular class, or regulator type, then normalize them into a structured table or workbook. Use when the user asks for lists such as transcription factors, pathway genes, receptor families, chromatin regulators, methylation readers, or other function-specific gene sets with identifiers and references. ([MDhewei/bioinfor-claw](https://github.com/MDhewei/bioinfor-claw/tree/HEAD/gene-list-analysis/curate-gene-list-by-function) · ★65)
- **gene-list-over-representation-analysis**: Use when you have a list of differentially expressed genes (e.g. DESeq2 output with gene name/id, log2 fold change, and p-adjusted values) and want to test whether GO terms or KEGG pathways are over-represented, with multiple-testing correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-list-over-representation-analysis))
- **gene-list-over-representation-analysis**: Use when you have a differential-expression gene list (up- or down-regulated DEGs), especially from a non-model species, and need to identify which KEGG/GO pathways are significantly over-represented. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-list-over-representation-analysis))
- **gene-list-overlap**: Compare 2 to 6 gene lists for overlap and enrichment. Computes pairwise Jaccard similarity, overlap coefficients, and Fisher's exact test for enrichment relative to a background. Generates Venn diagrams (up to 4 sets), UpSet plots, and an overlap matrix heatmap. ([MDhewei/bioinfor-claw](https://github.com/MDhewei/bioinfor-claw/tree/HEAD/gene-list-analysis/gene-list-overlap) · ★65)
- **gene-list-overlap-contingency-table-analysis**: Use when the query input is a list of genes (cluster marker genes with 'cluster' and 'gene' columns, or trait-associated genes) rather than an expression profile, and a cell type label is needed for each gene set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-list-overlap-contingency-table-analysis))
- **gene-loading-norm-ranking**: Use when you have a CA-embedded scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., Zhengmix8eq SingleCellExperiment with corral output) and need to identify which genes carry the highest weight in the first two CA dimensions and interpret their association with cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-loading-norm-ranking))
